Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The reactions variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 920 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 195 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 920 + O(1/n^195). Summing over all modes and applying the reactions convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
